- **wealth-app-m1-finance.portal**: Accesses automated investment portfolio data from M1 Finance via Playwright with Chrome cookie authentication. Configure email and Chrome profile in vault/wealth/config.md. Triggers: "debt review", "payoff timeline", "how much interest am I paying", "model extra payments", "debt summary". ([fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life](https://github.com/fru-dev3/AI-Ready-Life/tree/HEAD/paperclip-pkg/skills/wealth-op-debt-review) · ★17 · 2 copies)
